Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the combinatorics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 923. The resulting operator achieves bound O(710/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. This completes the proof. QED.
